I guess... well, this whole decline, it’s been going on since I started the community-college program. That was about two years and two months ago. Um, no, not that I've noticed. I mean, I hear people whispering sometimes, but that's just regular people actually whispering. Like when I used to go to the library. The librarian there was always shushing everyone, but she had this really loud shush, which is kind of ironic, you know? She wore these reading glasses on a little gold chain around her neck. My aunt had a chain exactly like that for her sunglasses, but she lost them at the beach a few years ago. The sand just gets absolutely everywhere when you go to the beach. We used to have this really long orange extension cord but my dad ran over it with the lawnmower. It made this awful crunching sound. But... yeah, no, I don't hear any voices or sounds that aren't really there.

Unusually what? Like, energetic or happy? Um, no. I've never really felt like that. I'm pretty much always just tired. Even if I try to drink an energy drink, it doesn't do anything. Well, yeah, I do know they talk about me. I mean, since I was in those community college classes, I've just known. The people running the school portal and the clinic practicum, they use these little wording changes in emails and assignment comments to mark me. They do it so everyone knows I'm someone they don't want in healthcare. My mom says they're just regular messages, but she doesn't read them the way I do. She reads her emails on her phone, and the screen is so small. I don't know how she sees anything. She has this cracked screen protector that she refuses to replace, and it has a bubble right in the middle. Bubbles in screen protectors are so annoying. It's like when you're putting up wallpaper and you get an air pocket, and you have to use that little plastic squeegee thing to push it out to the edge. My dad put up wallpaper in the hallway when I was little, and it had a pattern of little sailboats. Sailboats always look so peaceful, but I bet being on one is actually really loud with the wind and the sails flapping. The fabric on sails is really heavy, kind of like canvas. You could probably make a really sturdy tote bag out of an old sail. I need a new tote bag for my groceries, the paper ones always rip at the bottom if you buy anything heavy, like a jar of pickles. Pickles are so good, especially the spicy garlic ones. But yeah, the school portal people are definitely trying to push me out.

"Have you ever felt like your thoughts were being broadcast so others could hear them, or like thoughts were being put into your head or taken away?"

Um, no? I don't... I don't think so. My thoughts are just my thoughts, you know? They stay in my head. Sometimes my head hurts if I think too much, like when I was trying to figure out how to fix my headphones. The left side stopped working, and it's so annoying when you can only hear music in one ear. It makes the song sound completely different. I was listening to this one song by that band with the lead singer who always wears the big hats... I can't remember their name right now. But hats are making a comeback, I think. I saw a girl at the grocery store wearing a huge sun hat in the middle of winter. It was kind of weird, but also kind of cool. Maybe she was going on vacation somewhere warm. I'd love to go to the beach, the sand always feels so nice between your toes. But then it gets everywhere in your car, and vacuuming the car is such a chore. The vacuum at the car wash never has enough suction. It's like trying to suck up dirt with a straw. But, um, no, no one is putting thoughts in my head or taking them out. That sounds like a sci-fi movie or something.

========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
Um, unusual sensations? Inside my body? No, I haven't really felt anything weird like that. I mean, sometimes my stomach rumbles really loud when I'm hungry, like it's its own person. It happened during a test in high school once, during finals week, and it was so quiet in the room that everyone heard it. It was so embarrassing. Finals week was always the worst, anyway. I had this one history teacher who made us memorize all these dates for the Civil War, and I can never remember dates. I can't even remember my own cousin's birthday half the time. I think it's in October? Or maybe November. November is a weird month because it's right before Christmas, but it's not quite winter yet, so you never know what kind of jacket to wear. I have this really nice denim jacket, but the buttons are kind of loose. I need to sew them back on, but threading a needle is so frustrating. The thread always frays at the end. But, um... no, no strange feelings inside my body or anything. Just the normal stuff.

========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
Have I... like, frozen like a statue or something? Um, no, I don't think I do that. I move around fine. I mean, unless I'm playing freeze tag, but I haven't played that since elementary school. We used to play it on the blacktop out back, and if you fell, you'd scrape your knees so bad. The school nurse always had those really stiff, cheap band-aids that never actually stuck to your skin. They'd just peel off the second you started sweating. Sweating is so gross anyway. Summer is my least favorite season because it's just too hot, and the mosquitoes are absolutely everywhere. I got a mosquito bite right on my ankle last year and I scratched it so much it left a little scar. Scars are kind of weird, right? How the skin just heals back completely different. Like it remembers what happened. I wonder if trees get scars when you carve your initials into them. People always do that at the park near my house, just carving hearts and letters into the bark with pocket knives. It seems kind of mean to the tree, honestly, like they didn't ask for a tattoo. But yeah, no, I don't get stuck in weird positions or freeze up or anything like that.

Seen things? Like, things that aren't actually there? Um, no, I haven't seen anything like that. My vision is actually pretty good, I think. I haven't been to the eye doctor since I was in middle school, though. We went to this place in the mall, right next to that pretzel stand. Oh my gosh, the smell of those pretzels is so good. I always get the cinnamon sugar ones, but they put way too much sugar in the little paper bag and it gets all over your fingers. And then if you touch your clothes, you just have sticky sugar everywhere. My mom used to get so mad if I ate them in her car. ========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
Laughing? Like, when I'm not supposed to? I don't really know. I don't think I laugh much at all lately. Laughing takes a lot of energy, honestly. It's like you have to use all these muscles in your face and stomach. Speaking of stomachs, I tried to eat some soup earlier, but it was just... too hot. It was tomato soup. Tomato soup is always better with grilled cheese, but making a sandwich is just too much work right now. You have to get the butter perfectly soft so it doesn't tear the bread. Tearing bread is the worst, especially when it's that really soft white bread. My grandma used to make her own bread from scratch. She had this huge wooden bowl she used just for kneading the dough. Wood is such a nice material, it feels so warm compared to plastic or metal. I wish more things were made of wood these days. Everything is just plastic now, and it breaks so easily. Like those plastic hangers in my closet, they just snap if you pull a shirt off too fast. But um, no, I don't really find myself laughing. I just sit there mostly.

Strange or unusual... smells? Or tastes? No, I don't think so. I haven't noticed anything like that. Everything just tastes kind of bland lately, honestly. Like cardboard. Felt like... like someone else was making me do things? Or controlling me? No, I don't think so. I mean, my mom tries to tell me what to do all the time. She always wants me to clean my room or come downstairs for dinner. Dinner is usually around six, but sometimes it's later if she has to work late. She works at a bank. They had this really great section on astronomy. I loved looking at the pictures of the galaxies and nebulas. The colors are so bright, pinks and purples and blues. It's crazy to think about how big the universe is, and how small we are. Like little ants just crawling around. But no, I don't feel like anyone is controlling my thoughts or my body or anything like that. Just my mom being bossy, I guess.

I haven't really dated since high school. High school feels like it was forever ago, even though it wasn't. We had this big clock in the hallway that was always five minutes fast. Blank? Like, just stopping? Um, no, I don't think my thoughts ever just stop. They're usually going pretty fast, actually. Sometimes it's like a radio that's stuck between stations, you know?
